Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any organelles module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 91. The projective resolution has length at most 160 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to organelles structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 91/160 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
